G. Bleed Air Isolation Valve Installation
SUBTASK 36-13-04-160-001
(1) Make sure that the bonding surface of the isolation valve [6] is clean.
SUBTASK 36-13-04-420-001
(2) Put the isolation valve [6] through the keel beam access hole from the left side of the keel
beam.
SUBTASK 36-13-04-420-002
(3) Install the isolation valve [6] in the correct orientation between the crossover duct [3].
SUBTASK 36-13-04-410-001
(4) Install the couplings [5], as follows:
(a) Install both couplings [5] with the T-bolt aft and hanging straight down.
(b) Tighten the couplings [5] to 45 in-lb (5.1 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.2 N·m).
SUBTASK 36-13-04-100-001
(5) Prepare the parts for the bonding wire installation as follows:
(a) Use a stiff bristle non-metallic brush, STD-124, a sealant removal tool, SPL-768, or an
equivalent tool to remove all traces of the sealant on the washers, screw, nut, bonding
jumper and bonding tab used for the previous installation of the bonding wire.
(b) Use alcohol, B00130, to clean all the surfaces of the washers, screw, nut, bonding wire
terminal, and bonding tab until the wipes are no longer discolored.
SUBTASK 36-13-04-420-003
(6) Install the bonding wire [4], as follows:
(a) Position the bonding wire [4] blue terminal on the bonding tab on the isolation valve [6].
(b) Install the screw [7], washers [8], and nut [9].
36-13-04
(c) Tighten the nut [9] to 33 in-lb (3.7 N·m) - 40 in-lb (4.5 N·m) with a torque wrench of an
appropriate range.
(d) Use a intrinsically safe approved bonding meter, COM-1550, such as an Avtron Model
T477W bonding meter to measure the electrical resistance between the bonding tab and
the bonding wire.
(e) Make sure that the bonding resistance is not greater than 0.005 ohm (5 milliohms).
(f) If the boding resistance is satisfactory, then apply adhesive, A01076, to completely
encapsulate the fasteners.
1) Refer to SWPM 20-20-10 Paragraph 4.E.
(g) If the bonding resistance is not satisfactory, then the installation must be taken apart,
cleaned, re-installed and rechecked until it is satisfactory.
SUBTASK 36-13-04-420-004
CAUTION
MAKE SURE THAT THE ELECTRICAL CONNECTOR AND RECEPTACLE ARE
CLEAN WHEN YOU CONNECT THEM. DIRTY CONNECTORS CAN CAUSE
DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
CAUTION
DO NOT USE PLIERS THAT HAVE METAL JAWS TO TIGHTEN THE
ELECTRICAL CONNECTOR. DAMAGE TO THE ELECTRICAL CONNECTOR
CAN OCCUR.
(7) Use the teflon-jawed pliers, STD-664, to connect the electrical connector [2] to the isolation
valve [6].
(a) Remove the protective covers from the electrical receptacle of the isolation valve [6] and
the electrical connector [2].
(b) Connect the electrical connector [2] to the isolation valve [6].
1) Tighten the connector with the teflon-jawed pliers, STD-664.
SUBTASK 36-13-04-420-005
(8) If it was removed, install the air conditioning duct section [1] on the left air conditioning bay.

(9) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-4
Row Col Number Name

36-13-04

C. APU Check Valve Installation
SUBTASK 36-14-02-020-004
(1) Remove the cover from the APU pneumatic manifold [4].
SUBTASK 36-14-02-420-001
(2) Install the APU check valve [2] between the APU pneumatic manifold [4] ducts with the flow
arrow pointed forward.
SUBTASK 36-14-02-420-002
(3) Install the couplings [3] on the pneumatic manifold [4].
(a) Tighten the nuts of the couplings [3] to 60 in-lb (6.8 N·m) - 65 in-lb (7.3 N·m).
1) Make sure that the loop bands do not touch.
2) For the forward coupling [3], make sure that the clearance between the retainers is
0.050 in. (1.270 mm) - 0.310 in. (7.874 mm).
3) For the aft coupling [3], make sure that the clearance between the retainers is
0.050 in. (1.270 mm) - 0.410 in. (10.414 mm).
SUBTASK 36-14-02-790-001
(4) Do this leakage test for the APU check valve [2].
(a) Do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU,
TASK 36-00-00-860-803.
(b) Examine the APU check valve [2] for leakage at the couplings [3].
1) Diffused leakage is permitted, jet blast leakage must be repaired.
(c) Do this task: Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System, TASK 36-00-00-860-806.
